Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The algebraic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 499 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 874 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the algebraic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 499 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 874. QED.
